U.S. export controls remain a potential supply-chain  risk. SJ Semiconductor is currently
on the U.S. Entity List, which could restrict its access to certain U.S.-origin equipment,
components, software and technologies that require export licenses. While export
controls have historically focused more heavily on advanced front-end semiconductor

manufacturing equipment and SJ Semiconductor's exposure to restricted back-end
packaging equipment appears relatively manageable at present, the U.S. has continued to
broaden semiconductor-related controls. As advanced packaging becomes increasingly
critical to AI and advanced computing, there is a risk that future restrictions could expand
to cover additional advanced packaging equipment or technologies. If SJ Semiconductor's
overseas suppliers are unable to obtain required export licenses, or if localization of

relevant equipment and components progresses more slowly than expected, the company
could face disruptions to capacity expansion, production and R&D, potentially constraining
its technology roadmap and long-term growth.
Our estimates vs consensus
What's priced in? Since its listing on April 21, SJ Semi’s share price has been volatile,
initially rallying sharply through May 26 as strong investor enthusiasm around China’s
advanced packaging and AI supply-chain localization drove a significant valuation re-rating.

From late May through July 9, the shares entered a period of consolidation and elevated
volatility as investors digested the rapid post-IPO rerating and increasingly demanding
valuation, although continued enthusiasm around advanced packaging supported periodic
rebounds. The stock subsequently sold off alongside the broader global technology and
semiconductor correction in July, as concerns over elevated AI valuations and the
sustainability of the AI capex cycle drove a broad risk-off move across semiconductor

equities. Following SJ Semi’s 2Q26 results, with quarterly revenue growth slowing to c.2%
YoY and net profit declining 16% YoY; the stock fell  as results fell short of elevated
market expectations.

What is the market missing? We think the market is missing three factors:

• Intensifying competition which pressures the margin. OSAT peers were all
attracted by secular growth opportunities of advanced packaging. As peers ramp
up capacity, we expect a certain level of pricing competition. Therefore, our gross
margin assumptions are slightly lower than consensus numbers in 2028.
• HBM tightness and leading-node capacity ramp-up pace and yield. Although we
are constructive on China AI GPU shipment growth and local HBM ramp, any

hiccup in GPU production or HBM sourcing (local and foreign) will impact final
shipments and thus  constrain near-term growth and execution
• Over-estimation of 3DIC, HBM sourcing and CPO opportunities. We think
expectations for 3DIC, CPO and HBM outsourcing opportunities appear optimistic,
with the timing and addressable revenue pool likely overstated, for details, please
see  Debate 3: Besides 2.5D packaging, how much upside can emerging advanced-

packaging technologies contribute to China OSATs?

Peer comparison
We identify three peer groups for SJ Semi:  Execution needs to substantiate the high

premium over peers:
• OSAT peers:  We benchmark SJ Semi against domestic A-share OSATs and global
OSAT leaders, reflecting its core exposure to semiconductor packaging and testing.
However, SJ Semi's greater focus on wafer-level advanced packaging and 3D
integration supports a premium valuation versus conventional OSAT peers. We

### Page 35

M
Asia Pacific Insight
Morgan Stanley Research 35
expect the premium to narrow over the long term as the advanced packaging mix
increases at peers.
• China AI GPU thematic peers: We compare SJ Semi with China AI GPU players,
given its exposure to the domestic AI/HPC ecosystem through advanced packaging.
While not direct business-model peers, this group provides a useful valuation
reference for the scarcity premium and growth expectations attached to China's

localized AI compute supply chain. SJ Semi's 2027 P/S and P/E are higher than large
local GPU companies (Cambricon and Hygon) seems demanding, as the revenue
growth of GPU players is expected to be stronger.
• Back-end equipment: SJ Semi sits downstream as a manufacturing/service provider
rather than an equipment vendor, but both groups benefit from increasing
adoption of 2.5D/3D integration, fine-pitch interconnect and advanced bonding.
Overall back-end equipment valuations seem more reasonable vs. SJ Semi.

SJ Semi trades at 31x/24x/19x 2026-28E P/S,  far above OSATs (~3x/3x/2x) and equipment
peers (~11x/8x/8x), and much closer to China AI GPU supply chain names (~25x/15x/12x).
We think the current valuation already prices SJ Semi much more as an AI/advanced-
packaging scarcity asset than as an OSAT, so execution needs to substantiate that
premium.

best suited to capturing long-term value. We assume an 8.0% cost of equity (beta 1.09, risk-
free rate 2.0% and risk premium 5.5%), a payout ratio of 50%, a medium-term growth rate of
26.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 5.0%, all of which are in line with other China AI supply

chain companies under our coverage.

SJ Semi: Earnings Growth Outlook
Revenue growth outlook:
• We forecast overall revenue to increase at a 25% CAGR in 2025-28.
• We expect chiplet/2.5D packaging to increase at a 36% CAGR in 2025-28, because
advanced packaging has become an increasingly critical part of the AI GPU supply
chain and chiplet architectures and as heterogeneous integration gain adoption.
• We expect mid level wafer processing business  to grow at 10% CAGR (2025-28),

driven by rising advanced-node wafer volumes and increasing outsourcing of
bumping, probing and other mid-end processes as packaging complexity increases .
•  We expect SJ Semi’s WLP business to grow at a more moderate 5% CAGR over
2025–28, as this segment is more exposed to smartphone relate chips such as
Radio Frequency (RF), fingerprint chips.
Earnings growth outlook: We expect SJ semi's earnings to grow at a 34% CAGR (2025-
28), outpacing its sales growth. While the company still needs to invest heavily in capex to

expand its capacity and  R&D for technology upgrades, we expect operating leverage to
emerge as revenue scales significantly.
GM trend: We expect the company’s gross margin to improve from 31% in 2025 to 32% in
2028, due to an increasing contribution from chiplet/2.5D packaging which carries
structurally higher margins. We anticipate SJ Semi to maintain technology leadership in
advanced packaging and China AI GPU demand to remain strong for next 3-5 years.

Therefore, SJ Semi's gross margin could improve mildly as capacity scales, but partly offset
by increasing competition as peers also ramp-up capacity.

Valuation Methodology
Our price target is Rmb118.4: We derive our price target from a residual income model, as
we believe this methodology is best suited to capturing long-term value. We assume an
8.0% cost of equity (beta 1.09, risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 5.5%), a payout ratio
of 50%, a medium-term growth rate of 26.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 5.0%, all of
which are in line with other China AI supply chain companies under our coverage.

Our price target implies 11.0x 2026 book value per share,  2.3x  the local OSAT peer
average. We think  its premium valuation already discounts much of SJ Semi's technology
leadership and AI-driven growth.

Bull/bear case discussion

Our bull case value is Rmb200: stronger revenue growth with accelerated gross
margin expansion
We assume (1) >40% revenue CAGR in 2025-28 driven by stronger-than-expected
domestic AI GPU demand; (2) limited competition risk as peers struggle to ramp up
advanced packaging capacity; and (3) gross margin above 35% in 2026-28 thanks to
production scale effects and technology breakthrough.
Our bear case value is Rmb80: slower revenue growth with gross margin erosion amid

intensifying competition
We assume (1) <20% revenue CAGR in 2025-28 given slower-than-expected domestic AI
GPU demand; (2) significant market share loss in China’s advanced packaging market as
peers scale capacity; and (3) gross margin below 25% in 2026 and 2027 due to strong
price competition and underutilized capacity.

SJ Semi: Company Profile
Established in 2014, SJ Semiconductor  is one of the leading advanced semiconductor
packaging and testing companies in China, focusing on wafer-level advanced packaging and
mid-end wafer processing. The company is among the earliest domestic providers of 12-
inch bumping services and has expanded into wafer-level packaging (WLP) and Chiplet-
based heterogeneous integration solutions. SJ Semiconductor provides end-to-end

advanced packaging services covering bumping, wafer probing, wafer-level packaging,
2.5D/3DIC, and advanced heterogeneous integration technologies for high-performance
computing, AI, data center, automotive and mobile applications. The company completed
its IPO on the Shanghai STAR Market in April 2026.
Business overview
SJ Semiconductor specializes in advanced packaging and wafer-level manufacturing
technologies. Its major technology platforms include:

• Mid-end wafer processing, including 12-inch bumping, redistribution layer (RDL),
wafer probing (CP), TSV and hybrid bonding;
• Wafer-level packaging (WLP), including fan-in WLCSP and advanced wafer-level
packaging solutions;
• Chiplet heterogeneous integration, including 2.5D integration (TSV interposer),
3DIC and 3D Package technologies targeting AI accelerators, HPC processors,
networking chips and other high-performance applications.
Key Competitors

SJ Semi ranked number 9 in the global OSAT market in terms of revenue in 2025 with a
market share of 1.9%, according to Gartner.  Major international competitors include ASE
Technology and  Amkor Technology. Major domestic competitors include JCET, Tongfu
Microelectronics, Tianshui Huatian Technology, and Forehope Electronics.

Key   customers and COGS 
SJ Semiconductor provides advanced packaging services primarily for leading fabless 
semiconductor companies, foundries and HPC customers. As of 1H25, the top 5 customers 
accounted for 91% of sales, and top 1 customer Huawei accounts for 74% of total revenue.  
SJ Semi's revenue is primarily from China customers; overseas revenue accounted for 6% 
of total revenue in 1H25. 
Manufacturing cost accounted for 50% of COGS in 1H25. Advanced packaging requires 
high levels of clean room, precision, and automation, thus it requires a significant amount 
of advanced  packaging and testing equipment. This equipment and these production lines 
are typically highly expensive, so manufacturing cost usually accounts for a high 
proportion of the operating cost structure.

Shareholding Structure 
As of the 1H26 reporting, the largest shareholders were Wuxi Chanfa  Science &

### Page 46

Asia Pacific Insight
46
Technology Venture capital (9.39%), an industry investment fund backed by stated-owned
capital. SJ Semiconductor has no controlling shareholder.
SJ Semiconductor  was originally established in 2014 as a joint venture between
Semiconductor Manufacturing International Corporation (SMIC) and JCET Group,
specializing in advanced semiconductor packaging and testing. In 2021, both companies
sold their stakes in SJ Semi to a consortium of financial investors. The main reason for

these sales was strategic. SMIC stated that the divestment would optimize resource
allocation and allow it to focus on its foundry business. JCET exited its investment to
monetize its holdings and strengthen its balance sheet while concentrating on its core
packaging operations.

Glossary
2.5D: 2.5-Dimensional Integration. Advanced packaging architecture that places multiple
dies, such as AI processors and HBM, side-by-side on an interposer or equivalent high-
density interconnect platform.
3DIC: Three-Dimensional Integrated Circuit. Advanced integration architecture that
vertically stacks dies to increase compute density and shorten interconnects, but with
higher process complexity, cost and yield sensitivity.

ASIC: Application-Specific Integrated Circuit. Chip designed for a specific workload or
application, including custom AI accelerators.
Back-end: Back-end Semiconductor Manufacturing. Semiconductor assembly, packaging
and testing processes performed after wafer fabrication. Advanced packaging is making
these processes increasingly complex and equipment-intensive.
Bumping: Wafer Bumping. Process of forming conductive bumps on a wafer or die to
create electrical connections to another die, interposer or substrate.

C2W: Chip-to-Wafer. 3D integration approach in which individual dies are bonded onto a
wafer, allowing tested dies to be selected before stacking.
Chiplet: A smaller functional die designed to be integrated with other dies within one
package, enabling system performance to scale without relying solely on larger monolithic
chips.
CMP: Chemical Mechanical Planarization. Precision polishing process used to create
extremely flat wafer surfaces, important for advanced 3D integration and hybrid bonding.

CoWoS: Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate. Family of 2.5D advanced packaging architectures
that integrates multiple compute and memory dies through high-density interconnects.
CoWoS-L: Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate – Local Silicon Interconnect 2.5D architecture
combining an RDL-based interposer with local silicon interconnects, offering greater
scalability and design flexibility for larger AI packages.
CoWoS-S: Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate – Silicon Interposer 2.5D architecture using a

full silicon interposer to connect multiple dies.
CP: Chip Probe Electrical testing of individual dies while they remain on the wafer, helping
identify functional dies before downstream packaging.
CPO: Co-Packaged Optics Integration of optical communication components close to
compute or switching chips to increase interconnect bandwidth and reduce power
consumption.
CSP: Cloud Service Provider Large cloud-computing customer that operates data-center

infrastructure and is an important source of demand for AI accelerators and advanced
packaging.
Dicing: Wafer Dicing Process of cutting a processed wafer into individual semiconductor
dies.

### Page 48

M
Asia Pacific Insight
48
ECP: Electrochemical Plating Process that electrochemically deposits conductive metals,
typically copper, for structures including RDL and TSV-related interconnects.
EUV: Extreme Ultraviolet Lithography Advanced lithography technology used in leading-
edge semiconductor wafer fabrication.
Fab: Semiconductor Fabrication Plant Manufacturing facility in which semiconductor
wafers are processed and chips are fabricated.
Fabless: Fabless Semiconductor Company Semiconductor company that designs chips

but outsources wafer manufacturing to foundries.
Fan-in: Fan-in Wafer-Level Packaging Wafer-level packaging architecture in which
package interconnections remain within the footprint of the original die.
Fan-out: Fan-out Packaging Packaging architecture that redistributes connections beyond
the original die footprint, enabling more I/O connections and potentially multi-die
integration.
Fine Pitch: Fine Interconnect Pitch Small spacing between electrical interconnects. Finer

pitch enables higher interconnect density and bandwidth but requires greater
manufacturing precision.
Flip Chip: Flip-Chip Packaging Packaging method in which a die is flipped face-down and
electrically connected through bumps rather than conventional wire bonds.
Foundry: Semiconductor Foundry Manufacturer that fabricates semiconductor wafers for
chip designers and other customers.
Front-end: Front-end Semiconductor Manufacturing Wafer-fabrication processes used

to create transistors and circuitry before semiconductor assembly and packaging.
GPU: Graphics Processing Unit Highly parallel processor widely used for AI workloads
and a key source of demand for 2.5D/3D advanced packaging.
HBM: High-Bandwidth Memory Vertically stacked DRAM positioned close to AI
processors to provide very high memory bandwidth. HBM availability can constrain AI
accelerator and advanced-packaging output.
HBM3E / HBM4 / HBM4E: High-Bandwidth Memory Generations Successive generations

of HBM with increasing bandwidth, capacity and packaging complexity.
Heterogeneous Integration Integration of different types of dies, potentially
manufactured using different process technologies, into a single package or system.
HPC: High-Performance Computing Compute-intensive applications requiring high
processing performance and memory bandwidth, making them major users of advanced
packaging.
Hybrid Bonding: High-density bonding technology that directly connects metal

interconnects and dielectric surfaces between dies or wafers, enabling finer interconnect
pitch for advanced 3D integration.
Interposer: Intermediate layer connecting multiple dies within a package through high-
density electrical interconnects; commonly used in 2.5D packaging.

### Page 49

M
Asia Pacific Insight
Morgan Stanley Research 49
KGD: Known-Good Die: Die that has been tested and confirmed functional before being
incorporated into a multi-die package, helping reduce yield-related losses.
Logic Folding: Huawei's logic-stacking approach that uses vertical integration to shorten
signal paths and increase effective transistor density through system-, circuit- and
packaging-level innovation.
Micro-bump: Very small conductive bump used to create fine-pitch electrical connections

between dies in advanced packages.
NPU: Neural Processing Unit Processor optimized specifically for neural-network and AI
workloads.
OSAT: Outsourced Semiconductor Assembly and Test Semiconductor service provider
specializing in chip assembly, packaging and testing. JCET, SJ Semi, ASE and Amkor are
examples discussed in the report.
Panel-Level Packaging: Packaging approach that processes semiconductor packages on
large rectangular panels rather than conventional circular wafers, potentially improving

manufacturing efficiency and cost at scale.
RDL: Redistribution Layer Fine metal wiring layers that redistribute electrical
connections between dies, bumps and other package components.
Singulation Package / Die Singulation Process of separating individual semiconductor
dies or packages from a processed wafer or panel.
SoC: System-on-Chip Semiconductor integrating multiple system functions, such as
processing, memory control and I/O, onto a single chip.

SoIC: System on Integrated Chips TSMC's 3D integration platform for vertically stacking
semiconductor dies using high-density interconnect technologies.
TCB: Thermo-Compression Bonding Precision bonding process that uses heat and
pressure to connect dies, increasingly important for HBM and advanced 2.5D/3D
packaging.
TSV: Through-Silicon Via Vertical electrical connection passing through silicon, enabling
high-density connections through interposers or vertically stacked dies.

UTR: Utilization Rate Proportion of installed manufacturing capacity that is actually being
used. A key measure of advanced-packaging supply-demand balance.
W2W: Wafer-to-Wafer 3D integration approach in which two complete wafers are aligned
and bonded before being diced into individual devices.
Wafer Thinning: Wafer Thinning / Grinding Process of reducing wafer thickness, enabling
thinner dies and tighter vertical stacking in HBM and 3D packages.

WLCSP: Wafer-Level Chip-Scale Package Wafer-level packaging in which the completed
package is approximately the same size as the semiconductor die.
WLPL Wafer-Level Packaging Packaging process performed while semiconductor dies
remain part of the wafer rather than after individual die singulation.
XDFOI: XDFOI Advanced Packaging Platform JCET's advanced packaging platform used

### Page 50

M
Asia Pacific Insight
50
for high-density and heterogeneous integration applications.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 073: 260915_ubs_advantech

Source PDF: all report pdf/260915_ubs_advantech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-15
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Advantech
UBS Taiwan Summit 2026 key takeaways: Four
growth engines to drive robust orders
Upbeat FY26 outlook reinforced
Advantech delivered a positive message at the UBS 2026 Taiwan Summit, backed by an
upward-trending monthly book-to-bill (BB) ratio and accumulating design win projects
in its major market in the US. The company continues to see solid order placement, not
impacted by higher pricing. Advantech's component sourcing further improved QoQ to

support robust order momentum. On profitability, Advantech can pass on over 70% of
component cost increases. Despite positive operating leverage, management noted
possibly higher opex due to restricted stock award (RSA) expenses, which have relatively
higher amortization during the first few years, starting in 2027.
Leading indicators support robust order growth
Advantech's sustained order momentum is not impacted by higher pricing, with no
signs of client double booking. Some clients even offer 100% down-payments seeking

Advantech's on-time delivery. Management sees an upward trending BB ratio, healthy
at less than 2x.  Pipeline visibility is further reinforced by North American design wins of
US$444m in H126. Orders are mainly supported by three major end applications: 1)
factory automation (particularly AMRs and cobots), semi equipment (front- and back-
end equipment), 2) healthcare, 3) green energy infrastructure.   Management expects
those and edge AI projects to be the major  order drivers in the next three to five years.

Margin pressure to ease as component headwinds subside
Advantech’s GM fell to 37.7% in Q226 due to escalating component costs, particularly
across tech inputs such as GPUs, CPUs, MLCCs, memory and PCBs. After initial price
hikes in Oct 2025, Advantech has maintained bi-weekly price adjustments for board
products and monthly adjustments for systems in 2026, allowing it to pass on over 70%
of cost increases. To mitigate supply constraints, Advantech has signed LTAs and

qualified more alternative component suppliers. It has also worked through specialized
PCB bottlenecks. Its supply sufficiency has further improved in Q326 vs. Q226.
Management maintained  guidance of flat QoQ GM and OPM for Q326, while remaining
confident the long-term GM will recover to its historical 39–40% range and OPM will
continue to trend upward, driven by expanding operating leverage.
Valuation: Raise price target to NT$782; maintain Buy

We raise our 2026-28E earnings, mainly to reflect: 1) stronger-than-expected top-line
growth; 2) lower GM on continued input cost pressure and time lags for price increases
to be reflected in its P&L; and 3) possibly higher opex starting in 2027 on RSA expense
amortization. The RSA was  approved by the board but not yet issued, pending
regulatory authority approval. We raise our price target from NT$744 to NT$782 and
reiterate our Buy rating on the solid top-line momentum.

Company Description
Established in 1981 and listed on the Taiex in 1999, Advantech is a world-leading Taiwanese
IPC supplier. Its product offerings involve embedded boards and chassis, industrial computers
and controls, and after-sales services with applications spanning manufacturing automation,

healthcare, transportation, energy and networking industries. To serve the global market, it
has three manufacturing sites in Taiwan, Japan and China and direct subsidiaries in 27
countries.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target for Advantech on 30x 2027E PE.
Industrial PC sector risks include: slower global economic growth; uncertain trade tariff
negotiations; higher interest rates that could result in weaker capex investment; readiness

and supply chain bottlenecks of IT technology that could slow project execution; currency
movement; and changes in component pricing that could bring downside to profitability.
In our view, key company-specific risks include: 1) slower-than-expected edge-AI product
adoption; 2) competition in the non-AI IPC segment, especially in China; 3) rising component
prices; 4) NTD appreciation against the USD; 5) slower macroeconomic growth that could
negatively impact overall capex allocation; and 6) demand growth that could attract more

new suppliers to join the market, which may intensify competition and possibly bring price
pressure.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over

the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Global Unichip
2026 Taiwan Summit:  CPU momentum and
ADAS projects support 2027F outlook
FY26 revenue outlook revised higher on improved wafer support
GUC attended our 2026 Taiwan Summit on Sep 14th. August revenue of NT$5,840mn
(+1%MoM/+111%YoY) is tracking above at 73%/75% of UBSe/Street +15%/+11%
QoQ. Following better-than-expected wafer support from TSMC, management raised its
FY26 revenue growth guidance from 36% YoY to approximately 100% in late-July, with

turnkey revenue expected to grow by over 100% and NRE revenue by high teens %.
Management maintained its 2026 corporate GM target of 20%+, despite rising revenue
contribution from its top  CPU customer. OPEX is expected to increase by high teens %
YoY, due to the new Turing center and higher employee bonuses, but strong revenue
growth should still drive meaningful operating leverage.
CPU project supports near-term growth and deepen CSP engagement

CPU turnkey project for the top CSP remains the main growth driver in H226F,
supported by increased wafer allocations for both the CPU project and GUC’s other top
customers. While the current CPU project carries a relatively lower margin given leading
CSP's extensive in-house design efforts, it has also strengthened GUC's customer
relationships. This has helped it secure the next-generation dual-die  CPU project, which
carries higher content and ASP and  should see initial revenue from late-2026 with more

significant revenue contribution in 2027E. GUC is also pursuing ASIC  accelerator
opportunities with the top CSP customer and the other major CSPs, although
management does not expect meaningful turnkey revenue before 2028.
Broader customer exposure supports medium-term growth
Beyond the current CPU ramp, GUC sees incremental growth opportunities across
ADAS, other CSP CPU/ASIC business, and CPO. Chinese customers'  ADAS turnkey
revenue is expected to begin in late 2026F, followed by a its U.S. 3nm ADAS project in

H227F, with both carrying higher margins than the current CPU program, although the
U.S. customer's ramp will depend on TSMC's capacity support. For the other CSP
customer, management expects revenue contribution from both CPU and ASIC   to begin
in H226F and could become another key  revenue driver in 2027. It is also working with
this CSP customer for Gen 3 and bidding on Gen 4 project. Although  CPO remains at an
early stage, GUC and its partners plan for  tape out  on TSMC’s COUPE by end-2026,

offering longer-term margin support given GUC’s greater involvement in chip design.
Valuation: Maintain Buy rating with a P/E-based price target of NT$6,700
We rate GUC BUY with a PT of  NT$6,700, based on 50x average 2027-28E EPS (vs
historical 15-62x range), supported by 52% CAGR in earnings over 2025-30E. We
reiterate that GUC should benefit from Google CPU upside and multiple automotive
project ramps.

Company Description
Global Unichip (GUC) is a leading IC design service company in Taiwan, founded in 1998. The
company's business is to design chipsets for customers that do not have IC design capability,
with the goal of reducing entry barriers and risks for chipset design and shortening the time

to market. The company's target to enable more IC design for start-ups and system
companies is complimentary with TSMC, which would benefit from a growing customer and
application base, in addition to the company's existing IC design and IDM customers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Global Unichip based on a PE methodology.
Downside risks include a slower-than-expected business recovery for mature node, lack of
new project wins, and wafer capacity constraints.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 1

Poya  (5904.TWO)
Positives Mostly Priced In; Downgrade to Neutral 
CITI'S TAKE 
We downgrade Poya to Neutral from Buy following its strong YTD share price 
performance. Although operating momentum remains positive in the near-
term (we expect a strong Sep SSSG and solid 3Q earnings), we believe the 
rally has already reflected much of the benefit from robust SSSG YTD and 
margin expansion. Looking ahead, tough comparisons from 4Q26E may 
make solid SSSG harder to sustain, limiting earnings upside and potential 
weighing on stock sentiment. Meanwhile, Cosmed’s expanding rollout of 
Olive Young brands adds to competition pressure for beauty spending. We 
trim our DCF-based TP to NT$70, implying 18x NTM P/E vs. 5-year mean of 
17x. We view current risk/reward as balanced. 
Strong performance leaves less room for further upside — Poya’s shares have 
rallied 73% YTD, outperforming the benchmark’s 57% gain and broadly flat 
performance for the TW retail sector. This reflects strong operating deliver, including 
6-7% YTD SSSG and solid margin expansion supported by op leverage and 
headcount optimization. While the company may continue to grow earnings through 
store expansion and productivity improvements, we believe much of this outlook is 
already priced in. Further share price appreciation may require additional earnings 
upside, which could become more difficult to deliver if SSSG moderates. 
Tougher comp from 4Q26E could temper SSSG momentum — Poya’s YTD SSSG 
has benefited from 4-5% ticket growth, partly reflecting price inflation, as well as 
traffic growth 1-2%. We expect the comp base to become more demanding from 
4Q26E onward, increasing the hurdle for keeping the current growth rate. Slower 
SSSG could reduce incremental op leverage and limit further margin expansion. 
Given its YTD rally, a shift to positive but slower growth could weigh on sentiment. 
Cosmed’s Olive Young rollout raises competition pressure — Cosmed introduced 
four Olive Young, Korea’s leading beauty retail channel, proprietary brands in late 
Dec 2025 and planned to expand dedicated sections to 120 stores by end-2026E 
( ~20% of Cosmed’s total store count), increasing local access to popular Korean 
beauty products. Per Cosmed, PSD at locations carrying OY brands increased by 
MSD y/y YTD vs. MSD y/y down across its overall network, suggesting the brands are 
gaining customer interest. We believe broader availability and product selection 
could intensity competition for customer visits and beauty spending, potentially 
slowing Poya’s traffic growth and increasing promotional pressure.  (continued)

Angela Hsu AC

Poya  (5904.TWO)
16 September 2026 Citi Research

Poya (5904.TWO): Positives Mostly Priced In;
Downgrade to Neutral
Implications — We keep our earnings estimates largely unchanged for 2026-
2028E yet we trim our DCF-based TP to NT$70 from prior NT$74.5 mainly to factor
in a lower terminal growth of 4.5% (down from 5%). We also factor in its stock split
in Aug 2026. Our target price of NT$70 implies an 18x NTM forward P/E vs its 5-
year historical mean of 17x, which looks balanced to us.

We believe Poya’s store expansion and efficiency improvements may continue to
support earnings, but we see limited scope for further re-rating given the earnings
growth moderation (net profits +9% y/y in 2027E vs. +27% y/y in 2026E based on
our estimate) and its strong share-price gains to date. T ougher comparisons and
rising competition add uncertainty around the durability of SSSG momentum and
margin expansion.
We would prefer to see sustained traffic growth through the higher comp base,

clearer evidence of additional earnings upside or a more attractive entry point
before becoming more positive on the shares.

Bull/Bear: Poya  (5904.TWO)

### Page 6

Poya  (5904.TWO)
16 September 2026 Citi Research
6
Poya
Company description
Poya is the largest retailer of fast-moving consumer goods in Taiwan, focusing
on low-priced cosmetics, accessories, clothing and food products for females
aged 15-49. Poya had 470 stores in Taiwan at the end of 2025 and believes
that the market can accommodate up to 700-800 stores before reaching
saturation.
Investment strategy
We have a Neutral rating on Poya. We think YTD rally has baked in most

positives, including strong SSSG and solid margin expansion. While
fundamentals remain solid, tougher comparisons from 4Q26E could
moderate this year's 6-7% SSSG, lowering operating leverage and capping
further earnings upside. Meanwhile, Cosmed's expanding rollout of Olive
Young brands is intensifying competition for beauty spending. Although
Poya's SSSG has remained resilient, braoder distribution of these brands
could divert customer traffic and increase promotional pressure. We continue

to recognize Poya's execution in store expansion and ongoing efficiency
improvements. However, with these strengths largely reflected in the share
price and growth momentum facing a higher hurdle from 4Q26E onward, we
see a more balanced risk/reward.
Valuation
We derive our 12-month target price of NT$70 for Poya from a DCF valuation
method. Our assumptions include a terminal growth rate of 4.5% and WACC
of 10.1% (beta of 1.05; risk-free rate of 4%; risk premium of 7.0%; cost of equity

of 11.3%). Our target price implies a P/E of 18x on our NTM EPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: intense price competition among online retailers, slower store traffic,
margin dilution due to aggressive store expansion, and a soft macro
environment.
Key upside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: easing price competition among online retailers, stronger store

traffic, margin expansion thanks to strong cost control, and a stronger macro
environment.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

Macquarie Equity Research
18 September 2026
Technology
Alchip Technologies
Growth acceleration under way
Key Points
• Alchip's 3nm AI ASIC project ramped in 2Q26 and has driven strong
HoH and YoY growth from 2H26.
• We believe the 3nm AI ASIC ramp-up and next-generation tape-out in
late-2026 are key catalysts for Alchip shares.
• While we do not forecast additional CSP projects, the company is
"highly confident" it can secure another major CSP customer.
Expect 100%+ sales CAGR in 2026/27
• We believe share-price upside could be driven by: 1) the successful
ramp of its key customer’s 3nm AI ASIC (shipments from June), 2)
success in winning the key customer’s next-generation projects (tape
out in late-2026), and 3) other key projects, including networking-related
projects that could help drive 2027 and beyond sales growth, along with
multiple ADAS projects (the first major project ramped up in 1Q26).
• We maintain our positive long-term view on ASIC industry growth,
driven by the continued outsourcing of IC design services to Asia,
supported by a large talent pool and lower costs, and by Alchip's strong
competitive position and track record. We are not concerned about
Alchip's key customer's agreements with other semiconductor peers.
We believe these agreements highlight the large, growing TAM for the
customer's AI ASIC and related ecosystem. We also note that Alchip's
largest customer has invested in Alchip stock twice.
• Company remains bullish through at least 2029: During its 2Q26
analyst meeting in August, the company reiterated that sales should be
"at least in line with AI ASIC industry growth through 2029." The company
also expressed confidence in 3nm AI ASIC sales from June 2026 and in
the successful tape-out of its next-generation AI ASIC project before
year-end.
Earnings changes: We raise our 2026E/2027E/2028E EPS rep by
9%/27%/20% to reflect the higher scale of AI ASIC projects.
Valuation: We raise our target price 4% to NT$6,784, based on a 26x 2028E
PER, from NT$6,542, based on a 30x 2028E PER. Our new multiple is back in
line with the historical average and to reflect high customer concentration.
Catalysts: Sustained revenue growth from June; successful tape-out of a
next-generation project in 2H26.
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
As a key AI beneficiary with a strong track record of successful leading-
edge project ramps, we continue to believe in Alchip's long-term growth
outlook and remain positive on AI ASIC industry growth. Link to prior
Alchip research.

### Page 2

Macquarie Equity Research Alchip Technologies
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Project ramp-up delays or failure to reach mass production,
resulting in lower-than-expected scale.
• Order reductions from key customers, including potential
impacts from US semiconductor regulations, leading to
sales or earnings misses.
• Failure to secure key customers' next-generation projects.
Company Description
Alchip is a IC design service companies that assists IC design

and system companies to complete chip designs. Their
revenues come in two main streams: 1) NRE (non-recurring
engineering) which is the service provided starting with the
back-end design of the semiconductor to tape out at foundry.
2) Turnkey occurs when Alchip assists customers in placing
wafer orders to foundries (TSMC) and delivering final wafers to
customers.

Macquarie Equity Research Alchip Technologies
Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a reasonably negative
view on Alchip Technologies. The strongest style exposure
is Quality, indicating this stock is likely to have a superior
and more stable underlying earnings stream. The weakest
style exposure is Price Momentum, indicating this stock
has had weak medium to long term returns which often
persist into the future.
Macquarie Alpha Model: Key rankings

The Macquarie Quant’s flagship Alpha model is a dynamic
multi-factor model based on a staple of quant factors such
as value, momentum, revisions, quality, and risk.

M
Update
Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
Partnership with ams OSRAM
for Breakthrough Optical
Interconnect Solution
This partnership represents another effort for Bizlink in
diversifying its data interconnect solutions across both the
copper and optical space.
What's new:
• Bizlink announced it will partner with ams OSRAM on next-generation
optical interconnects for the scale-up network.
• The solution will leverage ams OSRAM's 850nm thin-film micro VCSEL

technology (in 25um pitch array) and Bizlink's expertise in fiber-array
integration, optical packaging, and volume production, featuring low latency,
lower power (0.25pJ/bit), wide and slow (32 Gbps), reliable (no failures after
2,000+ hours of testing under stressed conditions), scalable  and
connectorized architecture.
• A demonstration of this solution  will be showcased in the upcoming ECOC
2026 conference in Spain.
Our view:
• We think the industry continues to seek innovative ways of solving the

power consumption issue in data interconnects, including the relocation of
the optical engines (e.g., NPO/CPO) and the alternative light sources (e.g.,
micro LED and micro VCSEL).
• This represent another effort from Bizlink to diversify its data interconnect
solutions across both the copper and optical space, which could help reduce
the risk of technology disruptions.
• We thus see positive implications to Bizlink from this in the mid to long term,

though the revenue contribution might take time.
• Stay constructive on Bizlink for its multiple drivers in the AI deployment
theme, including data/power interconnects and semi cap equipment.

Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We think our target P/E multiple is justified by an

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 107: 260923_ms_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260923_ms_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? Kinsus (3189.TW, not covered) reported August 2026 profit per TWSE
regulatory requirements:
• August revenue was NT$4,810M (+6% m/m, +41% y/y). July and August
revenues accounted for 68% of the consensus 3Q revenue estimate.
• August pre-tax profit was NT$1,110M (+256% y/y), 51% of the consensus 3Q
pre-tax profit forecast.
• August net profit was NT$817M (+354% y/y), 55% of the consensus 3Q net

profit estimate.
Our thoughts:
Kinsus is one of the leading global IC substrate suppliers, with exposure to both ABF
and BT substrates and the highest BT revenue mix among Taiwanese substrate
peers. We see an encouraging read-through to Unimicron and NYPCB, both rated
OW, to the extent that the strength reflects improving underlying substrate
profitability. The results are consistent with our constructive view of ABF substrate
makers and continued margin improvement.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
August pre-tax margin reached 23.1%, vs. 14.3% in 2Q26.
With one month’s net profit equivalent to more than half of consensus quarterly
earnings, the disclosure suggests upside risk to Kinsus’ 3Q consensus estimates.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)

Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand

n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

短期運力供給受限，惟傳統淡季將近 
 3Q26 美線運價優於預期 ， 預估稅後EPS 4.48 元：Aug-26 營收 216.11
億元(+3.72%MoM)，月增主因受惠歐美線價量齊揚。元富預估陽明
3Q26 營收 646.92 億元(+40.9%QoQ)。獲利方面，儘管中東衝突推升
燃油成本，且貨量成長帶動變動成本同步增加，惟 3Q26 運價仍維持
高 檔 ， 故 上 修 毛 利 率 至 31.54% ， 稅 後 淨 利 156.37 億元
(+172.7%QoQ)，3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.48 元。 
 2026 年供需缺口相對收斂，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 8.17 元：展望
4Q26，需求面而言，歐線隨傳統旺季結束，終端需求動能轉弱，預期
運價將緩步回落；美線旺季需求亦近尾聲，惟受巴拿馬運河通行量下
降及中國十一長假前提前出貨支撐 ， 預期短期運價仍維持高原期。另，
9/24 川習會後關稅政策變化仍須持續關注。供給面而言，美伊衝突及
紅海通行風險仍存， 繞行好望角仍將限 制有效運力供給。 整體而言，
預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 8.17/3.44 元。 
 買進轉中立，目標價 68 元：評價方面，以 2Q27F BVPS 計算目前 PB 
0.6X，介於陽明疫情前區間 0.5-1.7X 下緣。假設股利分配率 4 成，預
估發放現金股 3.3 元，目前殖利率 5.4%，惟傳統淡季將近，評等自買
進轉為中立，目標價 68 元(2Q27BVPS PB 0.7X)。

個股報告 
陽明為全球貨櫃運力第 9 大航
商，市占率 2.2% 
 陽明成立於 1972 年 12 月 ， 實收資本額349.21 億元 。 截至1H26， 全球共6,407
位員工及 233 個代理行 ， 每週提供105 條定期航線服務 。 根據Alphaliner 統計 ，
截至 Sep-26，陽明為全球貨櫃運力第 9 大航商，市佔率約 2.2%。自 Feb-25
起，陽明所屬聯盟由原先 THE Alliance 改組為 Premier Allianc e。陽明 1H26
航線運費收入占比別：亞洲近洋線 13%，亞二線(含中東、南美、紅海、澳洲、
南亞) 12%，歐洲線(含西北歐、地中海、越大西洋、歐地) 39%，美洲線(含美
東、美西) 36%。 
以船隊規模而言，截至 3Q26 陽明共有 99 艘船，包含自有船共 65 艘，租入船
34 艘，總運力 77.3 萬 TEU。為持續追求汰舊換新計畫，陽明預計 2026 年將
交付 5 艘 1.5 萬 TEU LNG 雙燃料船。

Sources: Company，MasterLink

2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.64 元，低於預
期 
 陽明 2Q26 營收 459.2 億元(+18.8%QoQ，+18.8%Y oY)， 季增主因歐美運價上
升。獲利方面，儘管油價上漲、內陸運輸燃油附加費增加，且歐美貨量占比提
升使變動成本上升，惟毛利率 17.5%、營益率 12.4%，皆優於原先預估。業外
方面，受到認列匯兌損失 0.36 億元影響，業外收益 12.91 億元，低於原先預
估，稅後淨利 57.3 億元(+299.4%QoQ，+482%Y oY)，2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.64
元，低於原先預估之 2 元。

3Q 26 美線運價優於預期 ，
預估稅後 EPS  4.48 元  
 根據上海航交所 9/18 公告，上海出口集裝箱運價指數(SCFI) 3,687.83 點
(+0.7%WoW，+207.78%Y oY)，遠東-美線皆續揚，漲幅維持低個位數，分別
為遠東-美西線+3.01%WoW，遠東-美東線+0.95%WoW；歐洲相關則續跌，遠
東-歐線-4.72%WoW，遠東-地中海-5.27%WoW。另，波斯灣航線為本次中東
衝突主要影響區域，本週運價轉跌，-1.03%WoW。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (115)金管投顧新字第 016 號         Page 4 of 7 
個股報告 
陽明 Aug-26 營收 216.11 億元(+3.72%MoM，+56.17%Y oY，2026 年累計營
收+11.93%Y oY)，月增主因受惠歐美線價量齊揚。元富預估陽明 3Q26 營收
646.92 億元(+40.9%QoQ，+53.7%Y oY)。獲利方面，儘管中東衝突推升燃油
成本，且貨量成長帶動變動成本同步增加，惟 3Q26 運價仍維持高檔，故上修
毛利率至 31.54%、營益率 25.94%。假設匯率持穩，預估稅後淨利 156.37 億
元(+172.7%QoQ，+158.5%Y oY)，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.48 元。

Exhibit 9：近年新加坡海運燃油價格

Sources: Bloomberg(Sep-26)， Masterlink

2026 年供需缺口相對收斂， 預估
2026 年稅後 EPS 8.17 元 
 根據 Alphaliner Aug-26 預測，2026 年貨櫃供給年增 4.5%，需求年增 2.5%，
供給增幅仍高於需求，惟供需缺口較 2025 年逐步收斂。短期供給方面，目前
歐美港口以局部壅塞為主，如：9/23 歐洲鹿特丹港等待天數約 3 天、美國長灘
港約 6 天；中東地區則因地緣政治風險仍在，遠東-波斯灣航線運力供給受限，
支撐運價維持高檔；目前部分貨物改由印度西岸轉運後以陸路運往中東，使得
印度西岸港口壅塞，等待天數約 3-8 天。

展望 4Q26，需求面而言，歐線隨傳統旺季結束，終端需求動能轉弱，預期運
價將緩步回落；美線旺季需求亦近尾聲，惟受巴拿馬運河通行量下降及中國十
一長假前提前出貨支撐，預期短期運價仍維持高原期。另，9/24 川習會後關稅
政策變化仍須持續關注。供給面而言，美伊衝突及紅海通行風險仍存，儘管
Gemini 聯盟逐步恢復部分紅海航線，其餘航商短期跟進可能性仍低 ，繞行好
望角仍將限制有效運力供給。

整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 2,003.1 億元(+22.5%Y oY)，考量近期中東衝突
再度升溫，且歐美航線貨量占比提升帶動燃油成本增加，預估稅後淨利 285.1
億元(+66.8%Y oY)，2026 年稅後 EPS 8.17 元。展望 2027 年，考量新船陸續
交付 ， 供給壓力逐步增加 ， 且假設無新增關稅政策干擾 ， 需求回歸傳統季節性，
預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.44 元。

買進轉中立，目標價 68 元  評價方面 ， 以2Q27F BVPS 計算目前 PB 0.6X， 介於陽明疫情前區間0.5-1.7X
下緣。假設股利分配率 4 成，預估發放現金股 3.3 元，目前殖利率 5.4%，惟
傳統淡季將近，評等自買進轉為中立，目標價 68 元(2Q27BVPS PB 0.7X)。

風險提示  1) 燃油價格高於預期；2) 貨量及運價走勢不如預期；3) 匯率風險。

營收走勢圖

研究員/張書明 brady.chang@uni-psg.com

大量科技主要做PCB 機鑽與量測設備 ， 研究部維持強力買進投資建議 ，
係因 ： （1）總月產能已達 400 台並維持滿載 ， 預期今年出貨將超過1000
台，優於3 月法說展望；（2）TM 量測設備重要性與獨佔性不斷提升，
公司持續從 25 年 TM2 迭代至2H26 TM4 提升產品組合，預期拉貨比例
將從 8 台背鑽機對應 1 台 TM4 提升到 5：1，有望使公司市占率大幅
提升； （3）大量先進智慧在切入半導體大廠製程站點後，26-27 年也
將翻倍成長。綜上所述，預估26/27 年營收 97 與 137 億元上修到 125
與 229 億元（+146%/+83%YoY），稅後EPS 從 27 與 43 元上修到 28 與
70 元。研究部認為PCB 產業持續擴張，同時規格不斷升級。大量科技
位於客戶擴廠的風口浪尖，其量測技術與獨家產品成為公司提升市占
率與切入半導體的關鍵護城河，稼動率滿載的同時積極擴產。給予 27
年 22 倍 PE，目標價從 1220 元上修到 1540 元（2027 EPS*22PER）。

重點摘要： 
1. 公司簡介 
大量科技（3167）成立於 1980 年，總部位於桃園市八德區。
2004 年4 月興櫃，2013 年 10 月上市。主要提供 PCB 與半導體
設備，包含 PCB 標準鑽孔機、成型機與高階 CCD 背鑽機、CCD
成型機、TM 量測機，進行鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、鑽孔、塗
佈等；半導體設備包含 Metrology、AOI 與自動化設備即時量
測、檢測。台灣八德為總部，江蘇設有南京與漣水兩個工廠。
服務據點遍及中國、印度、東南亞及韓國、日本等地。主要客
戶為各大台系、中系 PCB 板廠， 競爭對手為德系 Schmoll（施
摩爾） 與中系大族數控。2025 年產品組合為 PCB 高階設備 
61%、低階設備 20% 與半導體 9%，25 年高低階出貨量約 4 : 
6。

2. 產品組合持續優化，2Q26 財報優於預期 
大量科技 2Q26 營收 28.6 億元 （+47%QoQ，+125%YoY） ，優於原
先預期 23.9 億元。毛利率 44.2%，優於原先預期 43.4%，顯
示高毛利產品佔比提升，產品組合持續優化。先前費用預估過
度樂觀，儘管費用從原先 2.73 億元調整至 4.97 億元（費用
率 17.4%），2Q26 稅後 EPS 仍達 6.10 元，優於原先預期。
2Q26 淨利率從 4 月 21% 一路月增至6 月達 33%， 稅後EPS 從單
月 1.55 元提升至 2.69 元。隨著訂單與出貨量持續墊加，公司
將逐月顯現營運槓桿帶來獲利大幅提升。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 合約負債與在手訂單續創新高 
大量科技目前主力產品為 PCB CCD 高階背鑽機，隨著 AI 晶片
需求量與規格同步成長，近年背鑽機設備量與精度需求暴增，
大 量 科 技 高 階 設 備 出 貨 量 成 長 陡 峭 。25 年 出 貨 501 台
（+196%YoY），26 年截至 3M26 已出貨 139 台，26 年訂單能見
度達 800 台。依目前營收推估 26 年出貨應已超過 3 月預期，
且公司當時稼動率已逐漸滿載，設備交期已從 3 個月拉長到 9
個月。因此，研究部預估26 年出貨量成長性有望接近 25 年水
準 。 針對未來潛力產品TM 量測機系列 ，TM2 於 25 年首度推出 ，
出貨 27 台，1H26 以 TM3 大平台單軸機為主，未來則以 TM4 大
平台四軸機為主，2Q26 開始接單並於 3Q26 陸續出貨，帶動單
價與毛利提升。26 年截至 3M26 已出貨 7 台，當時預期全年出
貨將超過 70 台。

圖一、 3M26 在手訂單能見度

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

公司設備接單後通常先進入備料與組裝帶動存貨提升，部分客
戶下單時會支付 20-30% 訂金形成合約負債，部分客戶出貨才
付款。最終完成安裝、測試與客戶驗收後一次認列營收並收取
尾款。目前下單到出貨歷時約需 2-3 季，出貨到認列再歷時約
1 季。公司合約負債從25 年底 7.5 億元成長至 2Q26 15 億元，
保守假設合約負債約略反應未來 3 季 20% 的在手訂單 ， 目前在
手訂單將逾 75 億元，已超過25 年全年營收水準，顯示需求強
勁無虞。

圖二、 合約負債與在手訂單持續墊加

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

4. 供需缺口將逐漸擴大，26 年迎來機鑽長期結構性擴張循環 
AI 伺服器 PCB 正進入從需求量與價值同步提升的結構性擴張
循環。市場普遍預期 25-27 年 NV AI Server 機櫃出貨量將從
2.5 萬櫃提升至 8 萬櫃，成長逾 3 倍，CAGR+79%。產品主力由
GB300 逐步轉向 Vera Rubin 。此外，單一 AI 機櫃 PCB 面積與
層數也將持續增加，從25 年 GB 22-24 層持續提升至 27 年 26-
78 層，SemiAnalysis 估計，VR 每櫃高階 PCB 面積約較 GB 增加 
2.3 倍 ， 後續 Rubin Ultra 持續 朝 更 高 層 數 Midplane ／
Backplane 發展，每櫃高階 HLC PCB 數量、面積及板厚皆呈結
構性增加。帶動機鑽孔數增加外，板厚、板硬也將使得機鑽單
位時間上升。同時 SerDes 從 112G 向 224G 演進後，殘銅精度
要求持續提升，CCD 背鑽與 TM 量測的滲透率也將同步增加。

在 Rack 數、PCB 面積／Rack、PCB 板層數、機鑽孔數、鑽孔時
間與背鑽滲透率均同步提升的情況下，25-28 年機鑽需求將倍
數成長，機鑽設備成為板廠擴產的一大瓶頸，因此不只板廠積
極擴廠，國內外機鑽廠也嗅到產業結構性轉變的趨勢，在營運
波動大的設備產業仍積極擴產。假設 26 年背鑽供應商滿載供
應並持續擴產，到 28 年仍將出現數倍缺口。

圖四、 背鑽需求倍數成長

資料來源：統一投顧整理

5. TM 量測機重要性持續上修成公司市占率提升關鍵 
公 司 近 年 推 出 內 層 深 度 量 測 設 備TM 系 列 （Inner Layer 
Thickness Metrology），解決 AI Server PCB 隨板層數、鑽
孔數及傳輸速率提升後，背鑽在殘銅精度要求提升的問題。
Rubin／224G 世代對殘銅精度要求由過去 ±3 mil 持續提升
至 ±2 mil 。TM 會先用探測棒逐片 360 度量測通孔內層銅層
實際深度，再將 Z 軸資料傳給背鑽進行加工。大量科技在量
測方面具有軟硬體技術護城河，目前同業尚無法效仿大量科技
的成熟度與良率，因此公司在未來世代產品極具有獨佔性。25
年首要推出以單軸 TM2 為主，1H26 為大平台單軸 TM3，2H26
後開始均為大平台四軸 TM4。 
公司 TM 量測機只能與公司高階背鑽機搭配使用，無法與同業
背鑽機兼容 ， 背鑽機有望透過TM 高獨佔性提升拉貨動能 。 過去
公司預期每 8 台背鑽機將搭配 1 台 TM 訂單，隨著 PCB 面積
與板層數成長恐面臨微翹曲問題，每孔的孔洞與深度差異化更
大 ， 加上板廠出現爆板等良率問題 ， 更加凸顯未來TM 量測的重
要性。目前拉貨比例已提高至約 1:5-1:6，預期精度要求將會
持續提高，若要提升良率與生產效率，TM 需求將有大幅成長空
間，公司更有機會藉 TM 的技術門檻反向提升高階背鑽市占
率，成為長期成長引擎。

圖五、 NV Rubin 精度要求 ±2mil 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

6. 目前總月產能已達約 400 台維持滿載 
大量科技目前據點落於桃園、南京、漣水與昆山。近兩年鑽孔
設備產能快速擴張。25 年總月產能 250 台，年底提升至 300 
台，隨 AI Server 帶動 CCD 高階背鑽機與 TM 量測機需求快速
放量，26 年初產能開始明顯吃緊。3 月法說公司提及 3 個廠
加外包總月產能約 350 台，其中自有約 300 台，外包約 30-
50 台。目前總月產能已達約 400 台，預計產能配置 200 台
高階背鑽、200 台標準鑽孔，再搭配 50 台外包產能。展望27
年，公司計劃 27 年初將外包產能持續擴至 80 台，27 年底依
需求最高達 150 台，同時透過人力與生產效率爬坡有望再精進
自有月產能。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

7. HDI 滲透率提升，HLC 亦將同步增加 
近年 HDI 滲透率持續提升至目前約 18-20%，AI GPU／ASIC 的
I/O 數量、頻寬與封裝密度快速提升，使傳統機鑽無法滿足
Fine-pitch 與繞線密度需求，帶動Compute Board 由 4+N+4 逐
步升級至 7+N+7。HDI 具孔徑小、 繞線密度高、 訊號路徑短等特
性，不過目前在壓合良率與製程成本仍具挑戰，因此目前滲透
率仍有上限，且 UBB、Switch、Midplane 及 Backplane 仍需要
高層數 HLC、大孔徑 PTH、Power/Ground 與 Connector 互連。
且 Compute board 改為 HDI，中間核心層仍將由 20-30 層持續
往上成長，板子越厚，機鑽加工時間越長。因此，外層 HDI 階
數增加與內部 HLC 層數將同步增加而非互相取代，不影響機
鑽 TAM 的成長。

8. 未來若導入 PTFE 有望再提升機鑽單位需求 
AI Server 高速傳輸由 112G 往 224G、448G 演進後，PCB 傳
輸損耗成為系統瓶頸 ， 傳統 FR-4／高階 Low-loss CCL 的 Dk、
Df 與高頻穩定性面臨更高要求 ， 產業開始評估以PTFE 與既有
高階材料混壓，取代部分傳統 Epoxy／Hydrocarbon 樹脂介電
層，尤其集中在高速訊號層，降低介電損耗與訊號衰減。PTFE
優點為低 Dk、極低 Df、耐高頻與電氣特性穩定，適合 224G 
以上高速傳輸 ， 不過材料成本 、 壓合與表面處理難度較高 。PTFE 
本體較軟、容易變形，不同材料又可能增加刀具磨耗。大量科
技已測試多種材料方案，未來 M8/M9、混壓PTFE 等高速材料導
入後 ， 鑽孔機本身架構不需大幅改變 ， 但鑽針壽命 、 加工參數 、
板厚、翹曲與內層偏移均更加複雜，且 PTFE 質地柔軟易變形，
單位鑽孔時間恐變慢，反而提高量測與機鑽需求，導入反而對
機鑽產業前景正向。

9. 大量切入半導體量測，未來有望翻倍成長 
大量科技技術優勢在於高精度物理量測 、AI 視覺控制 ，22 年結
合公司既有高精度設備、運動控制、視覺檢測與系統整合能力
延伸至半導體設備，23-25 年隨先進封裝擴產而快速成長。產
品包含 Metrology、AOI 與自動化設備，應用於前段晶圓製造、
中段 CoW 與後段 oS。隨 CoWoS 封裝尺寸放大 ， 翹曲導致厚度 、
高度差與邊緣等量化控制的重要性提高，大量過往 TM 量測機
在 PCB 殘銅精度量測達業界之最，公司除了檢測是否缺陷外，
還能判斷缺陷、翹曲程度，成為公司切入半導體檢測量測的關
鍵。公司已切入CoWoS 多項站點，同時亦布局 SoIC、FOPLP／

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

FoCoS、CoPoS 與 CPO。CPO Inspection Sorting Machine 功
能包含外觀檢查、清潔、分選與搬運，競爭優勢除檢測精度，
同時整合量測檢測與自動化功能。目前處驗證階段，預期最快
1Q27 有望出現明確訂單。  
25 年半導體部門主要客戶 Foundry 與 OSAT 約各半 ， 製程佔比
COWOS 75%、FOPLP 14%、SOIC 11%。預期 26-27 年維持翻倍成
長，營收佔比維持 5-10%。大量董事會已於 2026 年 3 月 31 日
通過將半導體事業分割至 100% 持有的子公司大量先進智能股
份有限公司 ，2026 年 8 月1 日半導體事業獨立成爲大量先進智
能，為往後上市櫃預作準備。

圖七、 半導體產品組合

資料來源：統一投顧整理

10. 26-27 年受惠產業趨勢與技術護城河有望使獲利翻倍成長 
大量科技 8 月營收 12.2 億元（+6%MoM，+153%YoY），營收達
成率 66%，符合預期。第二季淨利率已從21%持續提升到 33%，
隨著高階設備的訂單和出貨數持續墊加 ， 公司營收將逐月增加
顯現營運槓桿帶來獲利大幅提升。預期 3Q26 營收從 26.1 億
元上修至 35.9 億元（+26%QoQ，+154%YoY） ，大幅優於歷史高
點，說明目前需求龐大。預期3Q26 營收為，毛利率從提升至，
稅後 EPS 8.6 元。 
展望未來，目前總月產能已達400 台，且稼動率已滿載，產能
配比大約是 200 台高階、200 台標準再加 50 台外包標準鑽孔
機，因此預期今年出貨將超過一千台，優於三月法說，明年有
望再超過兩千台。TM 量測機部分，去年主要出舊產品TM2，今
年上半年出 TM3， 下半年開始將以新產品4 軸 TM4 為主 ， 此外 ，
依照客戶抽檢比例，目前拉貨比例將從 8 台背鑽機對應 1 台
TM4 提升到 5：1， 帶動單價與毛利成長 。 大量先進智慧在切入
半導體大廠製程站點後，預期26-27 年也將翻倍成長，營收佔
比達 5-10%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

綜上所述，原預期 26-27 年高階設備出貨約 1000-1500 台，
隨著財報中營收與在手訂單開出，加上供應鏈觀察可以發現，
26-27 年需求量將遠優於預期。因此研究部將26-27 年高階設
備出貨上修至 1400-2400 台。預估 26/27 年營收 97 與 137 億
元上修到 125 與 229 億元（+146%/+83%YoY），稅後EPS 從 27
與 43 元上修到 28 與 70 元。

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶已確定之 ASIC 合作案。欣銓 1H26 在取得不動產、廠房
及設備的現金流出達新台幣 70.07 億元，較 1H25 的 18.65 億元
大幅增加約 51.4 億元。預期 2H26 則隨龍潭廠機台陸續交付並
完成數據驗證後，進入產能爬坡期，營收逐步向上。在排除匯
率波動前提下 ， 獲利亦預計維持逐季成長的趨勢 。 預估欣銓2026
年稅後 EPS 9.94 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最重要的增
長引擎 。 公司與策略夥伴(晶圓廠 )合作的 ASIC 測試專案 07/2026
開始明顯增加，而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光
子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向上，
因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元 (2027 年 PER24X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.36 元： AI 相關需求佳和通訊相關訂單維持強
勁。欣銓 2Q26 營收為 45.34 億元， QoQ+13.81% 。營收依應用
別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 33.4% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 18.2% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 12.2% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.5% ； Storage 佔 10.8% ； PC/Consumers 佔
9.9% ；Memory 佔 5.4% ；其他 1.6% 。毛利率的部份， 2Q26 毛利
率為 40.17% ，增加 1.81 個百分點。 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.36 元。  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.73 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與
進場裝機後，已於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓
08/2026 營收增加至 17.94 億元， MoM+1.57% ， YoY+46.57% 。
此廠區主要與晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 合作，針對  AI ASIC 提供
測試服務。這批高階機台目前的稼動率極高，幾乎處於滿載狀
態，其量產將顯著貢獻 3Q26 營收，也會讓晶圓代工業務的營收
佔比在 3Q26 逐漸放大。此外，在傳統應用領域如車用 IC 及  
General MCU 方面 ， 由於客戶端在疫後的庫存已降至合理水位 ，
需求正逐步回升。預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.73 元。

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG （環境、社會、公司治理）相關政策與行動，並於官方
網站設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目
標。  
環境（ Environmental ）  
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi （科學基礎減碳目標倡議）承諾，強化減碳
目標與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會（ Social ）  
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理（ Governance ）  
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會（ BSI ）ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台
灣企業永續獎（ TCSA ）－永續報告獎金獎」，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊
揭露與社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議（ SBTi ）官方驗證。  
◼連續  9 年榮獲「公司治理評鑑前  5% 」的殊榮。  
◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

日月光投控 (3711 TT)  Buy

高階封測需求持續吃緊，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：AI 與 HPC 帶動的先進封測與測試需求成長快速，日
月光投控再度上調先進封測 (LEAP) 營收 ， 將超越先前設定35 億
美元目標， 2027 年 LEAP 營收有望再增加 1 倍；一般封測成長
率預估由先前 YoY+13% 上修至 YoY+20% 。整 體ATM 業務 2026
年營收預期 YoY+35% 。 EMS 營收略低於 YoY+20% 。預估日月
光投控 2026 年稅後 EPS 17.95 元。全球超大規模資料中心正加
速升級，為支撐具備多模態能力的複雜推論，晶片設計已大量
轉向由多顆小晶片與高頻寬記憶體堆疊的系統架構，使台積電
CoWoS 產能無不應求。日月光受惠台積電訂單外溢，加上大客
戶 AMD 的次世代 Venice CPU 架構開始放量，日月光的 FOCoS
出貨向上，將帶動營收續創新高，因此對日月光投控投資建議
為 Buy ，目標價為 862 元 (2027 年 PER35X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 4.71 元：日月光投控公布日月光投控 2Q26 營收
1,910.64 億元， QoQ+10.02% 、YoY+26.74% 。其中 2Q26 ATM 營
收 1,261.48 億元， QoQ+12.19% 、 YoY+36.28% 。優於日月光預
期 2Q26 IC ATM 營收 QoQ+9 ~+11% 。合併毛利率方面，產能利
用率還是主要影響毛利率的因素。日月光投控整體合併毛利率
增加 0.94 個百分點至 21.01% 。 2Q26 稅後 EPS 4.71 元。  
日月光預估 2026 年資本支出達 105 億美元左右： 高階測試產能
非常吃緊，配合客戶的需求，日月光投控再度增加 2026 年資本
支出 20 億美元，約達 105 億美元，其中 40 億美元將用於新廠
房建設 65 億美元用於設備， YoY+91% 左右。設備支出中，大約
56% 用於封裝， 40% 用於測試，其餘用於 EMS 和材料。整體而
言，大約 70% 的設備資本支出是用於先進技術，大部分將分配
給 LEAP ，特別是晶圓測試。  
折舊方面，雖部分折舊到期，但資本支出大量增加，日月光投
控預計 2026 年折舊費用 YoY+12~15% ，折舊費用將達到約新台
幣 750 億元以上。

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  日月光 ESG ：  
永續浪潮不斷， ESG 成為企業競爭力的關鍵，日月光擴大台灣先進封測布局
的同時，更持續耕耘與扎根企業社會責任，致力於發展全方位的綠色概念。
永續治理：日月光集團是負責任的企業公民，以積極的行動確保商業行為符
合專業及道德的最高標準。公司長期的經營與成功必須仰賴企業的永續發
展及其社會責任的體現。  
環境與綠色創新：日月光承諾持續增加資源再利用及減少溫室氣體與廢水
排放、廢棄物產生及化學品使用，以改善生態效益並保護環境。  
供應鏈發展：日月光秉持「積極、互信、誠正、自省」的核心價值追求持續
成長，對於供應鏈管理日月光與時俱進，從選商、運用策略執行採購品項、
供應商整合，到集結各領域資源建構平台與集團採購制度標準化，更將永續
供應落實到採購日常管理，規範供應夥伴遵守行為準則，以「價值導向」引
導供應商重視永續發展，促進產業創新。  
員工關懷與發展：日月光集團承諾提供給員工安全、健康及優質的工作環
境，確保人才多元化及保障人權，並使公司擁有高技能及經驗豐富的人力資
本。  
社會參與：日月光集團積極參與慈善行動、教育計畫和社會工作回饋社區，
將資源做最完善的分配，並發揮正面的社會影響力。日月光集團的社會參與
策略係以「環保公益」、「產學教育」、「社區營造」及「對外倡議」為四
大發展面向，於「集團永續發展委員會」下設置了「社會參與團隊 (Social 
Involvement Taskforce) 」來促動集團內社會參與相關行動，並依循 LBG 
(London Benchmark Group) 模式審視各個發展面向之相關投入、效益及其影
響，每半年進行業務績效檢視及報告。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

股價相對大盤走勢

信昌電 (6173 TT)  Buy

新世代 VR 出貨在即，營運將迎來新一波躍升  
投資建議 ：基於： (1) 新一世代 AI 伺服器電源瓦數與峰值電壓持
續攀升 ， 帶動高階MLCC 規格與用量爆發性成長 ； (2) 高階 MLCC
產能滿載且供不應求，漲價效應有望延續至 2028 年；(3) 新產能
將於 2Q27 陸續開出，帶動營收與獲利持續跳升等因素，投資評
等為 Buy ，目標價 403 元。  
受惠 AI 伺服器高功率元件訂單需求大增 ， 2Q26 財報表現優異 ：
信昌電 2Q26 營收 13.76 億元（ QoQ+35.3%/YoY+23.9% ），優 於
市場預期，主因受惠 AI 伺服器高功率元件訂單需求大增；毛利
率 28.84% （ QoQ+0.49ppts/YoY+5.24ppts ），主因 AI 比重提升
帶動產品組合持續轉佳。因營運規模擴大，費用率由前季 8.01%
降至 6.57% ，本業獲利 3.06 億元（ QoQ+48.1%/YoY+66.5% ）。
業外方面，因去年同期匯損影響處於低基期， 2Q26 歸屬母公司
稅後淨利 3.06 億元，稅後 EPS 1.79 元，獲利呈現大幅增長，整
體表現優於市場預期。  
新世代 VR 出貨在即， MLCC 用量呈現倍增，營運將逐季上揚：
展望 2H26，隨 著AI 伺服器自 GB300 世代向 Vera Rubin 與 Rubin 
Ultra 等新一代高壓（ 800V HVDC ）與高功率架構演進，系統對
高階 MLCC 需求呈現爆發性成長 ， 預估MLCC 總產值 VR200 較
GB300 增長 150% 以上 。 其中 ， 具備防板彎與高耐壓特性的Mega 
Cap （單價為一般產品 6~8 倍以上）以及高穩定度的 NP0 產品
出貨將顯著放量，上述產品毛利率顯著高於平均水準。信昌電
目前高階 MLCC 產能呈現滿載，公司表示 2H26 有望再度調漲
售價， 2026 年全年漲幅將達 30%（上半年已漲 10~15% ），預 估
信昌電 2H26 營收及毛利率將呈現逐季增長 ， 3Q26 營收為 16.21
億元（ QoQ+17.8%/YoY+53.2% ）、 4Q26 營收為 17.56 億元
（ QoQ+8.3%/YoY+86.1% ）。綜上所述，預估信昌電 2026 年營
收為 57.69 億元（ YoY+42.2% ），毛利率 29.94% ，較去年顯著
增長約 5.7ppts ，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 6.96 元，獲利將大幅提
升。 2027 年稅後 EPS 12.93 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG(Environment, Social, Governance) ：  
信昌電 (6173 TT ）秉持永續發展核心理念，將 ESG 願景融入公司治理與日
常營運，建構完善的組織架構與管理機制。公司設立永續發展委員會，全面
推動環境、社會與治理各項策略，確保永續資訊的透明度與合規性。在公司
治理與風險管理層面，信昌電致力於落實誠信經營，落實嚴謹的供應商行為
準則與廉潔承諾，並結合國際品質與管理系統認證（如 ISO 9001 、 IATF 
16949 、 IECQ QC080000 、 ISO 45001 等），持續精進營運體質。在環境保護
與綠色營運上，公司以介電陶瓷粉末研發與 MLCC 製造為核心，落實溫室
氣體盤查與廢棄物、水資源管理，並積極投資博德新能與博新能源，分階段
布局再生能源與儲能設備以邁向 RE100 目標。在社會共融與人才發展方面，
公司提供完善的薪酬福利、多元教育訓練與健康檢查，積極維護職場安全衛
生，同時透過華科事業群慈善基金會等機構長期投入聽覺照顧、偏鄉教育與
弱勢關懷等公益行動，展現企業公民價值。

公司簡介：  
信昌電 (6173 TT) 成立於 1990 年，並於 2002 年 4 月上櫃。公司隸屬於華新
麗華集團，母公司為華新科技，持股比例約為 43.1% 。信昌電為台灣少數具
備從上游介電陶瓷粉末到下游被動元件垂直整合能力的廠商。品牌為 PDC，
主要產品包括 MLCC 、 Chip-R 及介電陶瓷粉末。生產基地方面，台灣桃園
廠生產 Powder 及 R- Chip ，楊梅一廠主要生產 MLCC ，另建置楊梅二廠 (今
年投資 15 億，主要生產高壓 /低損耗，高溫穩定型  MLCC ）及六甲廠（去年
Capex 12 億投資粉末場，主要建置新一代自動化高效生產陶瓷粉末及開發
次世代粉材），主要產品包含：介電陶瓷粉末、晶片電容器 (MLCC) 、晶片
電阻器與電感產品。 2026 年上半年產品組合佔比為： MLCC 佔 68% 、晶片
電阻佔 16% 、介電陶瓷粉末佔 12% 、電感佔 2%及其他佔 2%。

4 群益投顧

信昌電現金減資 6%、 10/7 起暫停交易、 10/19 新股上櫃：  
信昌電（ 6173 ）於 8/28 公告，為提升股東權益報酬率，增加公司每股獲利
能力，擬辦理現金減資退還股本予股東，預計減資比率約為 6％，每股返還
股東 0.6 元現金。信昌電經 115 年 4 月 29 日董事會審議通過擬辦理現金減
資金額新台幣 1 億 272 萬元，銷除股份 1027 萬 2000 股，預計每仟股減少
約 60 股（即每仟股換發約 940 股）及退還現金約新台幣 600 元。舊股票將
於 10/7~10/16 號暫停交易，新股於 10/19 號換發上櫃買賣。

新世代 VR 出貨在即， MLCC 用量呈現倍增，預估營運將逐季上揚：  
展望 2H26 ，隨著 AI 伺服器自 GB300 世代向 Vera Rubin 與 Rubin Ultra 等
新一代高壓（ 800V HVDC ）與高功率架構演進，系統對高階 MLCC 需求呈
現爆發性成長，預估 MLCC 總產值 VR200 較 GB300 增長 150% 以上。其中，
具備防板彎與高耐壓特性的 Mega Cap （單價為一般產品 6~8 倍以上）以及
高穩定度的 NP0 產品出貨將顯著放量，上述產品毛利率顯著高於平均毛利
率，加上信昌電目前高階 MLCC 產能呈現滿載，公司表示 2H26 有望再度調
漲售價， 2026 年全年漲幅將達 30%( 上半年已漲 10~15%) ，預估信昌電 2H26
營收及毛利率將呈現逐季增長， 3Q26 營收為 16.21 億元
(QOQ+17.8%/YOY+53.2%) 、 4Q26 營收為 17.56 億元
(QOQ+8.3%/YOY+86.1%) 。綜上所述，預估信昌電 2026 年營收為 57.69 億
元 (YOY+42.2%) ，毛利率 29.94% ，較去年顯著增長約 5.7ppts ，預估 2026 年
稅後 EPS6.96 元，獲利將大幅提升。

展望 2027 年，高階 MLCC 持續緊缺，新產能於 2Q27 開出：  
展望 2027 年，隨著 AI 伺服器自 GB300 世代向 Vera Rubin 與 Rubin Ultra

5 群益投顧 
等新一代高壓（ 800V HVDC ）與高功率架構演進以及下一世代的 Feynman ，
系統對高階大尺寸被動元件的需求呈現爆發性成長。目前高階 MLCC 產能
持續緊缺，廠區產能利用率呈現滿載，在供不應求的市況下，有望推升
MLCC 持續調漲價格。由於 Mega Cap 以及 NP0 MLCC 的需求遠大於現有
供給，高階產能持續處於結構性緊缺狀態，研究部預估供不應求的狀況有望
延續至 2028 年。

目前信昌電高階 MLCC 產能呈現滿載，為因應強勁的市場需求，信昌電積
極擴產佈局，規劃中的楊梅二廠與六甲新廠均預計於 2027 年第二季完工、
下半年逐步放量。其中，楊梅二廠將專注生產高壓、低損耗及高溫穩定型
MLCC，而六甲新廠則負責建置新一代自動化高效陶瓷粉末與次世代粉材產
線。雙新廠的落成不僅能有效緩解高階產品的供給壓力，更透過粉末研發到
元件製造的上下游垂直整合，進一步優化成本結構、推升產品毛利率。綜上
所述，在新產能開出及漲價效應雙重利多發酵下，預估信昌電 2027 年營收
為 94.72 億元 (YOY+64.2%) ，因 AI 比重顯著拉升 ， 帶動毛利率拉升至 35.20% ，
2027 年稅後 EPS 12.93 元，獲利將進一步拉升。

投資建議：  
考量因新一世代 AI 伺服器的電源瓦數及峰值電壓持續攀升，將帶動高階
MLCC 規格及用量需求持續提升，預估 2027 年將會更加吃緊，基於產業供
需狀況強勁，投資評等為 Buy ，以 2H27~1H28 稅後減資後稀釋 EPS 為計算
基礎，給予 24XPER ，目標價 403 元。

無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

矽力*-KY (6415 TT) 
車載與資料中心為主要動能

永豐觀點 
矽力杰短期毛利率較為波動，不過隨資料中心與車載訂單加持 ，以及第四代製程平
滲透率提升，應有助於中長期毛利率回穩。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年稅後 EPS 11.59 元(原預估 11.41 元)，2027
年稅後 EPS 15.34 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營運拓展，研究部
維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

ESG 評析 
矽力*-KY 企業永續評鑑整體為 B 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面
向平均得分分別為 B+等與 B 等。

2

矽力杰為電源管理晶片大廠：矽力杰成立於 02/2008，為電源管理 IC 設計公司，
產品線包括 DC/DC 轉換晶片、電池充電管理晶片、過流保護晶片、靜電防護裝置
與 LED 背光驅動晶片、能源監控管理晶片等。矽力杰 2Q26 產品終端應用主要區
分為四大領域，包括消費電子(如平板電腦、STB 與 TV)36%、工業應用(如 LED 照
明、伺服器與安防設備等)35%、資訊產品(如筆電)9%與網路通訊(如路由器、手機
等)6%、 車載14%。 國際電源管理晶片設計同業與矽力杰產品類似者為TI、Maxim、
ON Semi 與 ADI，台系類似的參與廠商則包括致新、茂達等。 
3Q26 營收優於預期：矽力杰 08/2026 營收 24.16 億元(+2%MoM，+52%YoY)，
總計 07~08/2026 營收 47.7 億元，落於研究部原預估 3Q26 營收的 75%，優於
預期。矽力杰2H26 營運動能，若以各終端應用區分則：資料中心為最主要推升力
道 、 車載上升(新款晶片推出與市占率提升)、 消費電子上升(即便市場需求較為疲弱 ，
但因公司新款晶片推出，故反而仍能向上)、資通訊產品下滑(因記憶體報價上漲，
推高終端售價而影響消費力道)、 網通滑落 。 整體而言 ， 矽力杰法說時表示3Q26 營
收僅微幅上升，實屬偏為保守(畢竟 07/2026 開始漲價，又車載與資料中心動能依
舊較為強勢)，目前展望 4Q26 展望亦正向。只是公司仍認為 2026 年隨正負因子
交雜，毛利率較有變數(包括晶圓/封測成本上升、存貨依據庫齡政策而有損失或回
沖認列、 公司依據產品與客戶別而有不等的漲價措施、 第四代製程平台挹注與產品
結構變化等)，不過研究部認為隨 07/01 已開始進行漲價，理應能消弭部分製造成
本攀升的衝擊 ， 遂預估2H26 毛利率應能優於 2Q26 水準 。 研究部預估矽力杰3Q26
營收 71.2 億元(+18%QoQ，+49%YoY)，毛利率 48.21%，稅後獲利 10.16 億元
(-35%QoQ，+49%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.6 元，並預估 4Q26 營收 75.13 億元
(+5.5%QoQ，+39%YoY)。 
2027 年樂觀以待 ， 資料中心與車載為成長主軸 ：矽力杰(1) 2027 年營收樂觀以待 ，
主要由資料中心/車載/工業推升整體動能 。(2) 資料中心(包括主板/企業級 SSD/光
模組)目前營收比重 15%~16%，2027 年預期>20%比重，公司資料中心三大項目
比重依序為光通訊>SSD>MB，其中光通訊與 SSD 個別均為 mid-to-high single 
digit，MB 比重較低。公司過往以 400G 以下為主，今年 400G/800G 均已導入，
隨市占率逐步提升，又未來若主板核心電源出貨，更將進一步推動資料中心業務規
模擴大。(3) 車載則來自公司車載晶片供應品項增加，每台車的可供應的內含價值
擴大，並且客戶群攀升，除中國品牌上量外，也進一步拓展海外市場。雖現階段車

載市況較為疲軟，但矽力杰因中國客戶持續放量，又新款晶片持續導入，加以產品
種類較為齊全，方便客戶一次性採購，有助於矽力杰在中國車載市佔率攀升。 (4) 
儲能與光伏需求，以及充電樁應用，亦能拓展工業領域的需求。(5) 矽力杰藉由第
四代製程平台產能供給，以及拓展本身測試產能，並開拓新封裝供應鏈，均有助於
舒緩其產能緊繃的問題， 從而加速客戶導入， 公司維持今年底第四代製程平台滲透
率 20%(去年底滲透率僅個位數)，明年第五代製程平台亦將加入量產範圍。 
維持買進： 研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年營收 254.96 億元(+35%YoY)，毛利率
47.98%，稅後獲利 45.31 億元(+83%YoY)，稅後 EPS 11.59 元(原預估 11.41 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 333.2 億元(+31%YoY)，毛利率 49.08%，稅後獲利 59.94 億
元(+32%YoY)，稅後 EPS 15.34 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營
運拓展，研究部維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

資料來源：CMoney，玉山投顧

蔡志昇  
daniel1009-71660@esunsec.com.tw  
研 究  報 告                
晶心科(6533.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
組織調整完畢，靜待成效展現 
 投資評價與建議 
隨著RISC-V需求持續提升，公司開案數同步增加，惟公司短期仍以授權費為
主，且轉向大型客戶使得案件認列時程拉⻑，營收波動也較大。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26尚無客戶大型專案貢獻，推估營收季減約18%，4Q26將回復成⻑：來自
歐美車用客戶也開始貢獻授權金 ， 惟美系CSP客戶因大型專案墊高2Q26基期 ，
推估25Q3營收4.64億元，QoQ-18.1%，因人員裁撤，費用將明顯下滑，推估
約4.5億元，本業有望轉為正數，EPS 0.25元。展望4Q26，因季底通常為授權
金認列高峰，推估營收5.15億元，QoQ+11.5%，本業亦有望維持獲利。 
費用開始嚴控，有望自2H26起維持本業獲利態勢，2027年展望正向：隨著公
司轉向大型客戶專案與車用市場，因案件時程拉⻑和車用認證期較⻑，使得各
季的營收波動較大，展望未來，隨著歐美客戶車用產品開案完成，車用產品將
是主要成⻑動能，惟目前仍以授權費為主，雖消費性產品需求不佳，然在客戶
降低成本考量，以及網通產品為提升運算與資料傳輸的效能與彈性，如達發
(6526)已宣布擴大採用RISC-V ， 皆有助公司營運 ， 而費用自3Q26起開始嚴控 ，
本業有望開始維持獲利，2027年隨著案件數進⼀步增加，本業獲利將明顯提
升；整體而言，公司對於RISC-V未來發展趨勢仍樂觀看待。 
 投資風險 
先進製程等案件持續不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 公司簡介  
晶心科為全球RISC-V CPU IP龍頭廠，市占率約30%：公司成立於2005年，早期專注自有架構V3，2016年開始轉
為RISC-V架構之CPU IP授權 ， 目前授權費占比約65%為主要營收貢獻 ， 權利金仍有待時間發酵 ， 其中授權費>90%
來自RISC-V ， 權利金則以V3占90%為主 ， 2018年開始收取RISC-V權利金 ， 占比約10% ， 產品線以32/64 bit為主，
員工人數約400人(短期若無營收顯著增⻑機會，公司會維持此規模)，其中研發人員約佔80%，先前北美約100多
人的團隊已裁撤，客戶數超過400家，採用公司IP出貨之SOC已突破170億顆，根據SHD 2024年行銷報告，晶心
科在RISC-V供應商市占率約30%，為全球第⼀大廠，產品應用涵蓋AI、消費性、IOT、網通等，AI占比>40%為最
大應用，消費性占比隨市況波動較大，占比由過往最大逐步降至低於20%，IOT占比約15%並持續朝20%邁進，車
用占比仍為個位數，但多為歐美客戶，未來成⻑性佳，占比將大於10%，出貨地區以台灣40%與美國30%為主，
中國地區因競爭性較大，未來將降低此處營運力道，比重已低於20%。 
相較於x86架構（Intel和AMD的處理器採用）為少數仍使用CISC（complex instruction set computer，複雜指
令集）設計，ARM、MIPS與RISC-V等架構皆採RISC（reduced instruction set computer，精簡指令集）設計，
ARM架構受惠低功耗優勢及完整生態系統，使得手持式裝置甚至更多應用大量採用，但其閉源架構及高額授權費
與權利金，也促使開源免費且可自由修改設計架構的RISC-V需求明顯提升，惟目前生態系統仍有待時間發展，晶
心科為RISC-V創始會員 ， 也是最大供應商 ， 其餘具代表性公司包含SiFive 、 平頭哥 、 芯來(芯原於2025/09/11 100%
收購)等。 
| 營運概況  
2Q26認列進度佳，惟組織調整造成持續虧損：受惠美系CSP廠AI產品貢獻授權金，2Q26營收5.66億元，創新高，
QoQ+73.5%，其中授權金3.8億元，創新高，顯示公司新開案情形良好，因公司裁撤美國部門仍需支付資遣費，
費用6.7億元，亦同創新高，然未來費用將明顯下降，因此本業持續虧損，EPS -1.34元，低於預期。 
3Q26尚無客戶大型專案貢獻，推估營收季減約18%，4Q26將回復成⻑：來自歐美車用客戶也開始貢獻授權金，
惟美系CSP客戶因大型專案墊高2Q26基期，推估25Q3營收4.64億元，QoQ-18.1%，因人員裁撤，費用將明顯下
滑，推估約4.5億元，本業有望轉為正數，EPS 0.25元。展望4Q26，因季底通常為授權金認列高峰，推估營收5.15
億元，QoQ+11.5%，本業亦有望維持獲利，推估EPS 0.9元。

### Page 3

3
受惠RISC-V採用持續提收，2026年營運穩定成⻑，且費用開始嚴控，有望自2H26起維持本業獲利態勢，2027年
展望正向：隨著公司轉向大型客戶專案與車用市場，因案件時程拉⻑和車用認證期較⻑，公司營運規模仍不足以
支持營收穩定成⻑，使得各季的營收波動較大，展望2H26，1)隨著歐美客戶車用產品開案完成，車用產品將是主
要成⻑動能，惟目前仍以授權費為主；2) 隨著與更多CSP tier1客戶合作，公司推出頂尖設計CUSCO，效能更優
於先前的AX66，將鎖定AI與資料中心客戶，未來客戶將不只已知的M客戶；3)雖消費性產品需求不佳，然在客戶
降低成本考量，以及網通產品為提升運算與資料傳輸的效能與彈性，如達發(6526)已宣布擴大採用RISC-V，皆有
助公司營運，而費用自3Q26起開始嚴控，本業有望開始維持獲利，2027年隨著案件數進⼀步增加，本業獲利將明
顯提升；整體而言，公司對於RISC-V未來發展趨勢仍樂觀看待，中⻑期而言，SHD Group預估在MCU、AI加速器
與網通等應用推升下，全球RISC-V 晶片出貨將加速攀升，至2030年出貨量將達162億顆，晶心科將同步受惠。

⻑期產業趨勢正向，惟營運波動較大，仍有待成效展現，投資建議維持中立評等：隨著RISC-V需求持續提升，公
司開案數同步增加，惟公司近年成⻑性仍以授權費為主，權利金成⻑趨勢相對較緩，且轉向大型客戶與專案後，
案件認列時程拉⻑，營收波動也較大。
